Found 2026-10-06 reading the record while the comparison held the two backends' declarations equal. **Done 2026-10-08**: both backends answer `version`, the version's `source_ref` and `source_digest`, null for version 0, and `person_dimensions`, each `<Type>.<metric>.<dimension>` of the type's own and standard metrics whose value is an object of a type marking an actor identity, a tracked reference's interval value included, or an actor's id, in ascending order (`library-api.md` §6 settles the form; `flowrun.person_dimensions`, `metric::person_dimensions`); the runner's sandbox now honours `version`, keeping each version's source, which it had ignored. Held by the publishing comparison, three cases alike through both backends, by the comparison of every example and by a core unit test; each of twelve mutations of either backend's rule or version record fails them.
  - [x] **A publish report's `person_dimensions` is not computed**, in either backend, though the rule is now built (`publish-and-import.md` §2, ADR-0114). **Recommended:** fill it with every type's marks in the version under publish, the same `<Type>.<metric>.<dimension>` strings `declaration` gives, so a reviewer sees each per-person read the version declares; small, the rule being one call per type in each backend, and held by the publishing comparison. **Done 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation on the report's person_dimensions"): both backends fill it from the version under publish, read whether or not its impact can be, and no longer list it as not computed; `publish-and-import.md` §2 says so. A dry run of `service-v2.yaml` names exactly the marks the installed version's declarations give, alike through both backends, and every dry run's report is compared with them; each of three mutations fails it.
  - [x] **A derived amount summed over nothing is the integer 0 in the core**, where a duration's is now its zero (`core/crates/objectflow-core/src/metric.rs`, `typed_derived`): an amount's zero needs its currency's scale, which nothing at that point gives. No example reaches it; found 2026-10-06 fixing the duration's. **Fixed 2026-10-08 (D546)** ("continue"): the claim that nothing gives the scale was wrong, `value.rs`'s `minor_unit` giving a currency's and a decimal's kind its own; the core now types an empty sum's zero as money at the currency's minor unit and as a decimal at its scale, as the runner's service does, which the empty decimal shared unrecorded. Two tests of the publishing comparison, a claim with no lines and a batch with no lots, hold it alike, each failing with its half of the fix taken out.

## The core

The engine in Rust (ADR-0132), built slice by slice and judged by replaying the design's examples through the Flow Studio against it and the reference runner (ADR-0153). Each item is a block of the first slice.

- [x] **The workspace and its gates.** `core/`, the toolchain pinned, the lints, and `core/scripts/check`. Done 2026-10-04: Rust 1.99.0 pinned with rustfmt and clippy, installed by rustup with `--no-modify-path`.
- [x] **Values.** Absence, the scalar types, the core's fixed-point decimal and money, durations, timestamps and dates, under `declaration-syntax.md` §8.2 and §8.3. Done 2026-10-04 (`core/crates/objectflow-core/src/value.rs`), tested at the boundaries: half-to-even rounding, overflow refused, division by zero unknown, dates and instants round-tripped.
- [x] **The expression language.** The grammar of §8 and §9, parsed into an exhaustive tree, every expression of the design's modules parsing as `scripts/flowexpr.py` parses it. Done 2026-10-04 (`src/expr.rs`): all 280 of the design's expressions and 35 hand-written constructs give Python's tree, or fail where it fails (`scripts/dump-expressions.py`, `tests/parse_like_python.rs`).
- [x] **Loading a module.** The strict YAML loader and the model the first slice runs, every design module loaded. Done 2026-10-04 (`src/yaml.rs`, `src/model.rs`): all eleven design modules load with the enumerations, types, attributes, states, conditions, invariants and transitions the flow checker's loader reads (`scripts/dump-modules.py`, `tests/load_like_python.rs`); what the slice does not run is named on its type. Writing the test against Python corrected the model twice, each time to the schema's own definition of `unique`.
- [x] **Evaluating.** The expression language over a store's objects, three-valued. Done 2026-10-04 (`src/eval.rs`, `src/world.rs`): names resolved as the runner resolves them, unknown propagating but through `is null`, `and` and `or`, aggregates over sets and type scans, the operators' types; what the slice does not run (evaluator calls, metrics, intervals, `changed_since`) answered as not run. The replay against the runner is its full test.
- [x] **The store and a request.** SQLite as `storage-schema.md` lays it out, and a request on one object in the order of refusals. Done 2026-10-04 (`src/store.rs`, `src/request.rs`, `src/wire.rs`, `src/verdict.rs`): the directory, the log, the counters and a typed table per type, set tables, deferred foreign keys; a request walked through actor, object, transition, `not_requestable`, state, inputs, `_provided`, guards, outcome and every invariant, a refused one writing and allocating nothing, and what the slice does not run reached only where the request reaches it.
- [x] **The reads.** `get`, `history`, `events`, `declaration`, `availability`. Done 2026-10-04 (`src/reads.rs`): `availability` as ADR-0152 states it, a guard on an input waiting for it and one that would ask an evaluator listed as unevaluated.
- [x] **The service.** The wire of ADR-0150, the `/v1` routes and the sandbox's. Done 2026-10-04 (`crates/objectflow-service`): axum over the synchronous core, `--port 0` for a test, a construct the slice does not run a `501` naming it, an observation kind's declaration among them.
- [x] **The replay against the core.** The studio's replay of every example through the core's service, none failing. Done 2026-10-04 (`apps/flow-studio/server/tests/test_core_replay.py`): 21 of the 163 pass with the runner's outcome, none fails, 140 are not run naming what they reach, 2 wait for importing; a ratchet, `core_passing.txt`, fails the replay if any example the core passed stops passing (ADR-0153, Where the first slice stands). The later slices below raise the count; their figures are in ADR-0153's record.
- [x] **Identifiers minted from sequences.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/store.rs`, `src/request.rs`): a sequence drawn per scope in `of_sequence`, its format written, a refused request's draws kept as the format requires. No example passed on it alone, since those that mint also run effects.
- [x] **Effects and cascades.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`): a request taken depth-first, each caused transition through the same checks, guards and outcome, then its effect and its cascades, recording its own event; assign, clear, add, remove, create, call and loops in the design's order and limit; `call_refused` with its clause qualified by the type it targeted, a part's refusal qualified by the part's type, `over_limit`, `this_event`. The replay found three faults on the way: a set-valued reference with an opposite end stored as an empty set where the opposite derives it, an invariant's remedy blind to what an effect writes from an input, and both players sending a decimal as a JSON number where the wire carries a string. 55 pass.
- [x] **Supersession and a sandbox's evaluators.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`, `src/eval.rs`, `src/reads.rs`): `superseded_by` written by a supersede step and `successor_not_superseded` checked (ADR-0144); the sandbox's answers asked by a guard, an unanswered one refusing `temporal` (ADR-0138), an absent argument asking nothing (ADR-0146), an offer asking none (ADR-0048). A sum of amounts is an amount. 76 pass.
- [x] **The interval index.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/store.rs`, `src/request.rs`, `src/eval.rs`): `of_interval` written with each event, a span for the state and every tracked member on a creation and for each changed value after; `intervals`, `intervals(<member>)`, `entered_at` and `time_in` read from it, with ADR-0146's and ADR-0148's durations. The replay corrected the closed stretch's start while a transition into a closed state is taken. 97 pass. `entered_at(STATE)` reads the span, where the runner keeps a state's last entry time apart; the two could differ only after an external transition from a state to itself, which the format forbids and the flow checker now refuses (D518).
- [x] **Observation kinds.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/model.rs`, `src/request.rs`, `src/eval.rs`, `src/reads.rs`): a kind expanded into the type `declaration-syntax.md` §6.8 says it is sugar for, its declaration served; `record` through the request path with `corrects_current`, `subject_open` and a correction's subject checked, a correction keeping the occurred time it replaces; a subject's collections read as its uncorrected observations; recording offered on the subject. A sum over no elements now meets a duration or an amount as its zero. 122 pass.
- [x] **Shared state machines.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/model.rs`): a binder takes its machine's states, conditions and transitions at load, its own initial replacing the machine's, once every module is read. 138 pass.
- [x] **A request's occurred time.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`): every transition a request takes dated by its occurred time, held by `occurred_within` and the interval check; and `whole_open`, which the core had not evaluated, now refusing a part moved into a finished whole, held by a test since no example reaches it. 145 pass.
- [x] **The attribute write index.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`, `src/store.rs`, `src/eval.rs`): `of_attribute_write` set by inputs, effects and changed values, a part's transition on its whole's part relationship and a recording on its subject's collection; `changed_since` read from it. 149 pass.
- [x] **Assertions.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`, `src/store.rs`): a requested assertion's `to` and `admits` checked, its state set and its event `asserted`; admissions kept in `of_admission`, a standing one skipped, an admitted one recorded with its reason, and one whose invariant holds again discharged. A caused assertion is still not run. 152 pass.
- [x] **Erasure.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`, `src/store.rs`, `src/reads.rs`): personal attributes cleared, observations forgotten, the supersession chain erased, past payloads and interval values redacted, an invariant it breaks admitted, and `not_erased` refusing a later write in a request and an offer. 156 pass. Not yet: the taint walk into callers' and caused events (`storage-schema.md` §9, steps 3 and 12), and files, legacy entries, proposals, external ids and labels, which the core does not hold.
- [x] **`referrers`.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/eval.rs`): every object holding a stored reference to this one, its parts excepted. 157 pass.
- [x] **Families.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/model.rs`, `src/world.rs`, `src/request.rs`, `src/store.rs`): a subtype inherits its bases' attributes, derived attributes and invariants; scans, references and uniqueness count the family. The slice found D519 in the reference runner and D520 in the storage design. 158 pass.
- [x] **Proposals.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`, `src/model.rs`): the built-in `Proposal`; filing checked short of the state and the guards, its occurred time bounded at filing; `approve` executing the held request as the approver, the proposal's event first, a refusal leaving it pending; `reject` and `withdraw`; expiry; an erasure invalidating a pending proposal on its object and redacting its inputs. The slice found D521 and D523. All 161 examples that run pass, the other 2 waiting for importing as against the runner.
- [x] **Corrections recorded as such.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/request.rs`): the core had not read `corrects`, so a correction's event carried `observed`; it now carries `corrected`, and a correction of a tracked value is not run until its relabelling is. Found D522.
- [x] **The import.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/import.rs`, `crates/objectflow-service`, ADR-0154): `import_batch` over mirror types through the built-in assertion, one transaction per batch, classes found and applied as the disposition file decides, legacy keys and external identifiers in `of_external_id`, `entered` and creation times, `recorded_from`, refreshes, sequences raised, dry runs; `lookup`; `state_source` written on every state change, which the core had never written; a recording on a mirror's object refused as `subject_owned`. Both studio backends serve it, and every one of the 163 examples passes against the core and the runner, none waiting. Not yet: legacy entries; since done, legacy intervals and silent spans (2026-10-05), and a refresh's intermediate values and observations and labels on a mirror's objects (2026-10-06).
- [x] **Publishing, first slice: the change and its dry run.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/publish.rs`, `src/source.rs`, ADR-0155): `of_declaration` with versions 0 and 1, every event and object carrying the version installed; the built-in `DeclarationChange` and its transitions, `draft` and `revise` taking the source and its reference and recording its digest, held to `check-flows.py --digest` over every design module; `submit` and `refresh` attaching the dry run's report and refused while it finds anything fatal; `publish` with `dry_run`; the impact report's categories, each object named, the migration applied in simulation and every invariant evaluated after it. A sandbox's publish took its caller's checks and outside a sandbox it was not run, until the core's checker was done the same day (C4 below).
- [x] **Publishing, second slice: the approval.** Done 2026-10-05 (`src/store.rs`, `src/publish.rs`, ADR-0155): `publish` refused as `stale`, `fatal`, `impact_unchanged` or `decision_unresolved` against its dry run recomputed at the attached report's `now`, and otherwise installing the version in one transaction: tables rebuilt around the mappings with foreign keys off and checked before the commit, renames and set-asides, one migration event per object under the version it migrates from, `admit`'s admissions, newly tracked members' intervals opened and dropped ones closed, a newly personal attribute redacted on erased objects (D527), the other open changes superseded, the proposals it breaks invalidated, a dropped type retired; observations left as they were. `Stale` is a verdict, and the wire's `publish` answers it. `draft` refuses a file under a name it does not declare; a version that would leave a personal value beyond erasure is not run. Proven by five tests and through the service; `current` is not reachable in a sandbox, where every open change is superseded at a publish.
- [x] **The core's own checker** (ADR-0156), on the declaration model, each slice held to `scripts/check-flows.py` by file, line and code over every design module and every exported plant, and joining the dry run's report as it lands; its four slices are done, the checks neither checker enforces remain: **Done 2026-10-09**: the inventory below is empty, every check of §10 enforced in both checkers, subsumed (30) or withdrawn (4, 45, 63), and ADR-0172 keeps the reference's internal form as the core's oracle.

Done 2026-10-05: `check-flows.py --export-plants` records every check its run makes, 208 over the design's modules and its self-test, into `core/crates/objectflow-core/tests/plants`, and its run without arguments fails when they are not current. Exporting found that `schema_errors` reported a step's error at the step, the branch error's path being doubled under `jsonschema` 3.2.0; it now reports the branch error's own line, held by a plant;
  - [x] C1, steps 1 to 3. Done 2026-10-05 (`src/check/`, `tests/checker_oracle.rs`): the strict load, the schemas, a module's by the core's draft-07 validator and an examples file's, YAML literals given their expression text, the expressions parsed, the imports and the names a module's closure declares, then the names and relationships of a module over its view, its migration's rules and its checks against the previous version, and the notices beside them, each function named as the one it ports; every finding of a module agrees with the Python checker's over all 208 checks, by file, line and code, and the oracle fails when one check is broken. The core's loader now refuses a `?` in a plain scalar inside `{ … }` or `[ … ]`, as PyYAML does. The dry run runs them, its report carrying each finding as `check:<code>` with its file and line, and goes on past a fatal one where the rules can still run (ADR-0156 decision 5, revised); the publish tests' modules are now valid descriptions, clean under the Python checker;
  - [x] C2, the sixty-four checks of `declaration-syntax.md` §10. Done 2026-10-05 (`src/check/model.rs`, `data.rs`, `lines.rs`): [`docs/design/core-checker.md`](docs/design/core-checker.md) inventories them, which are the model's and which the notation's, and which step 3, the text checker, the schema or neither enforces; `check-flows.py` gained `syntax_plants`, 31 YAML plants for the 22 checks its plants did not reach; and the core runs every model check the text checker enforces, each re-derived from the YAML's meaning and reported at the YAML path the conversion maps its line to, in the text checker's words, which its deduplication keys on. Step 4 runs only when no file has a fatal finding, over every module together. All 36 step-4 findings over the 245 checks agree by file, line and code, with no extra one, and the oracle fails when a check is broken. Where a check quotes its line, the core builds that line's words for the message alone;
  - [x] the checks neither checker enforces, found by the inventory on 2026-10-05: none whole (4 withdrawn by ADR-0171), and an expression's types beyond an evaluator's verdict (24), which the expression parser accepts; each needs the reference first, so the core has something to be held to; **Check 12 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 12"): both checkers refuse transitions causing each other in a cycle, by a `call` or a `create` in an effect, a loop's included, or a part's cascade clause, a subtype's for the parts it inherits included, and a transition causing itself, at the step or clause closing the cycle, under step 3's `names` (`check-flows.py` `cascade_cycle_errors`, `check/names.rs`); every module of the design stays clean. The flow checker's self-test holds seven cases exactly, which the core's oracle replays; each of fifteen mutations of either checker fails them, one only once the oracle counted findings (D545). **Check 32 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 32"): both checkers refuse an invariant that reads the clock, `now`, `time_in` or a span's `duration` (§8.3 names `time_in`, §6.9 runs a current span's duration to now), directly or through a derived attribute of its type or of another reached through a path, typed by step 3's walker, under `names` (`check-flows.py` `clock_errors`, `check/names.rs`); an observation kind's invariants stay check 54's. No invariant of the design reads the clock, scanned and checked. The self-test holds seven cases exactly, `entered_at` and a derivation reading no clock accepted, which the oracle replays; each of thirteen mutations fails them, none a build failure. **Check 30 subsumed 2026-10-08** (ADR-0170, "go with your recommendation on check 30"): the flow format refuses every cascade to an abstract part type, since the type declares no transitions, which is more than check 30 refuses; observed running a whole cascading to an abstract part type every member of whose family declares the transition, refused by both checkers with the same finding, and now stated in `flow-format.md` §4.14 and held by a self-test case the oracle replays, beside one that survives instead, accepted. **Check 10 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 10"): both checkers refuse a personal value reaching a non-personal attribute, by a write, by an argument of a `call`, a `create` or a cascade clause that the transition reached writes there, however many calls on, or by a backfill, under step 3's `names` (`check-flows.py` `personal_errors`, `check/names.rs`). A personal value is an input marked so, an attribute input of a personal attribute or one the effect writes into a personal attribute, as the runner's `input_personal` has it, or a personal attribute or observation field read anywhere in an expression, its own object's, another's through a typed path, or a derivation's; step 3's walker now records each member it reads, walks a presence test when asked, and types a bare observation collection, the fixtures confirming no other check's findings change. Every module of the design stays clean; an existing plant, a step made conditional on an optional input, gains a second, genuine finding. The self-test holds sixteen cases exactly, which the oracle replays; each of thirty-four mutations of either checker fails them, none a build failure, two only once function arguments were planted. **Check 36 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 36"): both checkers refuse a re-parent, an external or internal transition writing a part's end back to a whole whose type is abstract, by an `assign` or by naming it among its required or optional inputs, under step 3's `names` (`check-flows.py` `reparent_errors`, `check/names.rs`); a creation writing it, a re-parent to a concrete whole and a write of a plain reference to the abstract type are not refused. Its first clause, a part/owner pair whose types disagree, was already refused as ends that do not name each other, now held by a case. No module of the design re-parents to an abstract whole, the fixtures unchanged by it. The self-test holds seven cases exactly, which the oracle replays; each of sixteen mutations of either checker fails them, none a build failure. **Check 5 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 5 and 4"): both checkers refuse an invariant reading `inputs` or `this_event`, which no write after it has, as step 3 already refused them in a derived attribute or a uniqueness condition (`read_errors`, now with an invariant's members apart from a condition's); every other read fitting no form already has a check of its own (probed 2026-10-08: an evaluator refused as a guard's alone; the actor, the clock, a metric and a label by checks 64, 32, 56 and 55), and `referrers` is a traversal. Four cases of the self-test, which the oracle replays; each of four mutations fails them. **Check 6 enforced 2026-10-08** ("go with your recommendation, start on 6"): both checkers refuse a type-scan invariant that fails the swap test of §3.4, run over the whole invariant with its own derived attributes read as their expressions, naming the member read on one of the two objects only, and a scan excluding the object itself by its id or by `this`, under step 3's `names` (`check-flows.py` `symmetry_errors`, `check/names.rs`); §3.4 now says the test runs over the whole invariant, which its worked examples showed and its text left unsaid. No invariant of the design is refused. Ten cases of the self-test, which the oracle replays; each of eighteen mutations of either checker fails them, none a build failure. **Check 47's body enforced 2026-10-08** ("continue", on the recommendation of checks 47 then 2): both expression parsers refuse a `sum`, `min`, `max`, `avg`, `median` or distinct `count` with no body, as §8's grammar has it, so it is step 2's `syntax` finding, worded alike (`flowexpr.py` `BODIED`, `expr.rs`); §8 and the §10 row name `avg`, `median` and the distinct count beside the three it named, the value of each being its body. All 552 of the design's expressions still parse. Held by both parsers' tests, the core's pinning the reference's words, and a plant the oracle replays; each of six mutations fails them, none a build failure. **Check 2's types enforced 2026-10-08** ("continue"): both checkers refuse an argument of a `call`, a `create` or a condition's evaluator call whose value does not fit the input or argument it is given to, where both kinds can be told, with step 3's own value typing and check 17's fit (`argument_type_errors`, `evaluator_argument_errors`, in `check-flows.py` and `check/names.rs`). No module of the design is refused; `group_erasure_plant`'s two retyped modules gain genuine findings, their own assertions holding. Seven cases of the self-test, which the oracle replays; each of twelve mutations of either checker fails them, none a build failure. **Check 24 enforced 2026-10-09** ("pushed, continue", on the recommendation of §8.3's operator set with conservative inference): both checkers type each expression of a module, a condition, an invariant, a derived attribute, a uniqueness condition, an effect's values and a backfill, inferring a type from literals, members and inputs, paths through typed references, aggregates over typed collections and §8.3's functions, and refuse a rule of §8.3 broken where every operand it reads is known: money of two currencies or decimals of two scales added, an int and a decimal added, two timestamps added, money times money or a duration times a duration, a scalar over a quantity, a comparison of two types, membership in an enumeration of another type, a connective or `not` on what is no bool, a conditional of two types, `length` of what is no string, and a condition, invariant or uniqueness condition that is no bool; one finding an expression, the first in evaluation order (`check-flows.py` `Typing`, `expression_type_errors`; `check/names.rs`). The inference types 715 of the examples' 921 subexpressions and decides 83 of their 142 comparisons and 15 of their 17 arithmetic operations, and the design is clean. Known limits, in §10's row: a metric's expressions are not typed, date arithmetic and a timestamp times a scalar are left, and two states, or an identity and a string, count as comparable. Nineteen cases of the self-test, which the oracle replays; each of thirty-four mutations of either checker fails them, none a build failure. **The inventory is now empty**: every check of §10 is enforced, subsumed (30) or withdrawn (4, 45, 63).
  - [x] **`authoring-flows.md` says no check asks whether a condition can be true** (§ on the checks, "None asks whether a condition can be true. A search of the record for satisfiability, contradiction or a solver finds nothing"), which step 5's contradiction analysis (ADR-0131, ADR-0137) has made untrue. Found 2026-10-08 searching the design for check 6's status. **Recommended:** a dated correction in place, saying step 5 now decides contradictions within one type. **Done 2026-10-08** ("continue"): the study's §1, "What the design has today", is annotated as the design of 2026-09-25, since several of its bullets have dated, the flow format having brought descriptions and dropped hand-written versions, and the sentence itself marked as holding until step 5.
  - [x] **Check 4 refuses the design's own invariant, and the reason it gives no longer holds: a decision for the author.** Built 2026-10-08 (saved aside, not committed), check 4 refused `unit-journey.md`'s `ServiceJob.engineer_active`, `not open or engineer.state == User.ACTIVE`, which reads through `ServiceJob.engineer`, a reference to `User` in `operations_shared` with no opposite end; D453 put the invariant there on purpose (2026-09-28), since a guard on `User.leave` would make the modules import each other, and declaring the far end would too. Check 4's reason, that a write to the far object "cannot find the invariant to re-check" (§3.4), dates from before ADR-0056: one end of a relationship is now stored and its inverse a derived view over it, and "a stored end is always available to a filter" (§3.1, §11 question 5), so a write to a `User` finds the jobs naming it through their stored end, declared opposite or not. Both backends enforce it today: the example `an_engineer_with_an_open_job_does_not_leave` is refused through both. **Recommended:** withdraw check 4 by an ADR, as checks 45 and 63 were, stating that a traversal invariant is re-checked through the stored end of every relationship it reads through, whichever side stores it; and keep §3.4's three forms, a traversal costing an indexed lookup on the stored end. Rejected: enforcing check 4 as written, which refuses D453's invariant with no other way to state it; and requiring the far end declared only within a module, which leaves the same rule unenforceable across modules, where D453 needs it. **Decided 2026-10-08 (ADR-0171)**, "go with your recommendations on 1 and 2": check 4 is withdrawn, a traversal invariant reads through any relationship end, and the documents stating the old rule say so; the code built for it was discarded.

Found 2026-10-05 reading the snapshot while adding the log. *(Measured 2026-10-06 by `core/crates/objectflow-core/examples/probe_scale.rs`, a release build on an in-memory SQLite store of one type, five events an object, the median of nine runs: at 100, 500, 1,000, 2,000 and 4,000 objects a request took 3.0, 15.6, 34.9, 66.1 and 149 ms, a `get` of one object 1.9, 9.1, 19.3, 38.8 and 83 ms, a query about the same, and a metric read 5.5, 29.8, 60.7, 126 and 276 ms. Every operation costs at least one load of the whole store, about 20 µs an object, a request about two and a metric read about three, so the cost grows with the store's total size, closed objects and history included, and not with what the request reads; the first consumer's 700-odd live objects and their years of closed ones put a request in the tens to hundreds of milliseconds. Whether that meets C5, whose target is unset, and how reads should reach only what they need, are the author's to discuss; one machine, no disk and no PostgreSQL, so indicative only)* **Proposed 2026-10-06 for the author's discussion: ADR-0162 and `docs/design/scoped-reads.md`**, recommending reads through the database by what the rules reach, in three measured steps, and a C5 target for the author to set **Decided 2026-10-07 (ADR-0162), steps 1 and 2 built the same day**: the log, the attempt log and the rollup read on first use and an idempotency key's record by the key; objects read by id on first use and held, a scan reading its type, and with the scoped invariant check (the entry below) and identifiers, legacy entries and imported positions read by key or on first use, a request takes 0.3 ms and a `get` 0.1 ms at every size the probe runs, against 149 and 83 at 4,000 objects at the start (`docs/design/scoped-reads.md` §7); step 3, scans and metrics by index, remains, a query at 4,000 objects taking 40.7 ms and a metric read 226.7, of which about 170 ms is computed after its rows are read (`docs/design/scoped-reads.md` §7, "Before step 3"), and **the author decided 2026-10-07 (ADR-0164 decision 3)** that step 3 begins with the evaluator: rows shared and a row's member read by the evaluator's own rule took a metric read at 4,000 objects from 226.7 ms to 122.0 (`docs/design/scoped-reads.md` §7, "Step 3, begun with the evaluator"); a query's filter now reads only the objects in the states it requires, through each type's state index, 44.4 ms to 24.7 at 4,000 objects; a uniqueness and an external identifier read by their key, which took a request on a type with a unique attribute from quadratic, 108.9 ms at 1,000 objects, to 0.5, and with each uniqueness key indexed (ADR-0165) 0.4 at every size to 4,000 (`docs/design/scoped-reads.md` §7, "Uniqueness by its key"); a rule's type scan reads only the objects in the states its filter requires, a guard scanning working tickets at 4,000 objects 52.3 ms to 33.1, and its conjuncts on stored attributes narrow the read by their columns as well ("A rule's scan by its states"); a windowed metric over closed history has no index to read by range, the interval indexes being partial to current intervals, and **needs the author**: an index over every interval by dimension and entry time is a change to `storage-schema.md` §5, recommended only once a metric's time is measured to be its reads, which today it is not (`docs/design/scoped-reads.md` §7); and an object's record beyond its row is read on first use, a type's records together where many are reached (ADR-0165 decision 2): a query at 4,000 objects 24.3 ms to 6.7, a metric read, which reads every record of its family, 2 to 9% slower at every size for reading rows and records apart ("An object's record on first use");
  - **a request checks the invariants of every object**, not those its writes could violate: step 7 in `core/crates/objectflow-core/src/request.rs` (the loop over `ctx.work.in_order()` after `take`) and the reference runner's (`scripts/flowrun.py`, the loop over `work.objects` after `take`), where `DESIGN.md` §5.6 and ADR-0045 check "every invariant the written objects could violate" and ADR-0055 makes that set computable from each invariant's form, local, traversal through declared inverses, or a symmetric type scan. The two agree wherever every violation in the store is admitted, which every route into the store keeps, and the whole-store check is now what a request reads beyond its own reach: by the probe a request grows by about 9 µs an object, which that read accounts for by inference, not by a measurement of the step alone. Recommendation: the core computes the affected set from each invariant's reach, a superset of what it reads, and the replays and the backend comparison hold it to the runner's answers. Step 7 now checks the objects the request changed or made and every object of a type whose invariants reach them, by a classifier that takes a declared invariant reading only its own object as local, a uniqueness or external identifier as reaching the families it scans, and anything else as reaching any object, a kind's invariants on the recording alone (`request.rs`, `invariant_reach`, `affected_by_writes`); `request::tests::the_invariants_a_write_could_break_are_checked_and_no_others` holds a traversal and a uniqueness, each part mutation-tested;
